rgba(0,0,0,0.3)\\n- Rounded corners: 12px border radius\\n\\n### Interaction States\\n- Default: static card display\\n- Hover (web): subtle scale animation (1.02x)\\n- Tap avatar: show upload options with user name\\n- Upload button accessible via screen reader\\n- High contrast mode support\\n- Minimum touch target 44x44px\\n\\n## User Stories\\n\\n**US-1**: As a user, I want to upload a profile photo so I can personalize my account\\n**US-2**: As a user, I want to see my profile information in a clean card layout\\n**US-3**: As a user, I want confirmation that my photo uploaded successfully\\n**US-4**: As a user, I want to remove my profile photo if I choose\\n\\n## Out of Scope\\n- Video profile uploads\\n- Animated GIF avatars\\n- Multiple profile photos / photo File Validation**\n   - Client-side: MIME type check (image/jpeg, image/png)\n   - Server-side: Magic number validation (not just extension)\n   - Size limit: 5MB enforced at API gateway and application layer\n   - Reject executable file types (.exe, .sh, .bat, etc.)\n   \n   **B. S3 Security**\n   - Private bucket ACLs (no public read)\n   - CDN as sole public access point (Origin Access Identity)\n   - Server-side encryption (SSE-S3 or SSE-KMS)\n   - Versioning enabled for audit trail\n   - Object lock for regulatory compliance\n   \n   **D. Authentication & Authorization**\n   - JWT/session token required for all upload endpoints\n   - Verify userId in token matches userId in URL path\n   - Prevent users from uploading to other users' profiles\n   \n   **E. Rate Limiting**\n   - 5 uploads per hour per user (sliding window)\n   - IP-based rate limiting: 20 uploads/hour\n   - Return 429 Too Many Requests with Retry-After header\n   \n   **F.
